Projection algorithm:
def project_feasible(x,s):
  x=np.asarray(x,float)
  low=np.min(x); high=np.max(x)
  # target s
  for iter: mid=(low+high)/2; y=np.clip(x-mid,0,1); ysum=y.sum(); if ysum>s: high=mid else low=mid. 60 iterations enough. Then y. If residual small adjust.
But if x all same? if sum target within [0,n]; yes.

Define smooth_objective(x, beta, lam):
  c=correlate(x)
  maxc=c.max(); vals=np.exp(beta*(c-maxc)); logsum = maxc + log(vals.sum())/beta
  penalty = lam*(x.sum()-s)**2
  return logsum + penalty
Gradient:
  weights = vals/vals.sum()
  g = weighted_grad(c or weights)
  g += 2*lam*(x.sum()-s)
Return g.

Projected backtracking with exact F:
Pseudo:
def projected_gradient_step(h, beta, best_c, max_iters, eta0, time_limit):
  f = eval_c(h)
  eta = eta0
  for it in range(max_iters):
    w, grad = weights_grad(h,beta)
    # maybe use gradient of exact max if beta large?
    if norm(grad) small: break
    improved=False
    for _ in range(30):
       h2=project(h - eta*grad)
       f2=eval_c(h2)
       if f2 < f - 1e-14 or f2 < best_c - 1e-14:
          h=h2; f=f2; best_c=min(best_c,f2); improved=True; break
       eta *= 0.5
    if not improved:
       # try random perturbations along gradient and random directions
       # maybe decrease beta/increase?
       # if no improve with tiny eta, break
    eta = min(eta*1.05, eta_max)
  return h,best

Objective:
```
def _opt_obj(x):
    _OPT['calls']+=1
    if _OPT['calls'] % 50 ==0 and time.time()>_OPT['time_limit']: raise _TimeStop
    beta=float(_OPT['beta'])
    c = _correlate_full(x)
    if beta <=1e-8:
      val = c.max()
    else:
      m=c.max(); vals=np.exp(beta*(c-m)); val=m + np.log(vals.sum())/beta
    s=_OPT['s']
    penalty=_OPT['lam']*(x.sum()-s)**2
    # maybe slight box penalty? bounds handle
    return float(val+penalty)
```
Gradient:
```
def _opt_grad(x):
    _OPT['calls']+=1
    beta=...
    c = _correlate_full(x)
    if beta <=1e-8:
       # gradient of max (subgrad): active weights uniform over exact max
       weights=np.zeros_like(c); active=(c==c.max()); weights[active]=1/active.sum()
    else:
       m=c.max(); vals=np.exp(beta*(c-m)); weights=vals/vals.sum()
    g=_weighted_gradient(x, weights)
    g += 2*_OPT['lam']*(x.sum()-_OPT['s'])
    return g
```

Weighted gradient function:
```
def _weighted_gradient(h, weights):
    n=len(h); off=n-1
    p_pos = weights[off:off+n]  # d=0..n-1
    p_neg = np.zeros(n) # index d
    for d in 1..n-1: p_neg[d]=weights[off-d]
    q = p_pos[1:] + p_neg[1:]
    # source weights
    pos_cum = np.cumsum(p_pos)
    neg_cum = np.cumsum(p_neg) # p_neg[0]=0
    source = pos_cum.copy()
    idx = n - np.arange(n) - 1  # for j=0 -> n-1? Wait we need sum l=1..n-j-1.
```
Let's derive indexing. For each j, neg contribution sum_{l=1}^{n-j-1} p_neg[l]. Let t = n-j-1. If t>=1, sum first t entries of p_neg[1:] (excluding 0), i.e., neg_cum[t] if neg_cum[0]=0 and p_neg[0]=0? Suppose neg_cum[k]=sum_{l=0}^k p_neg[l]. p_neg[0]=0. Need sum_{l=1}^t = neg_cum[t]. So source = pos_cum + neg_cum[n - np.arange(n) -1]. For j=0, t=n-1 => neg_cum[n-1] all neg. For j=n-1, t=0 => 0. Good.
    p0 = p_pos[0]
    # neighbor
    q_vec = np.zeros(n); q_vec[1:]=q
    if n > 800: use fftconvolve else np.convolve
    left = _convolve(q_vec, h,'full')[:n]  # q_vec length n, h n; full length2n-1.
    q_rev = q_vec[::-1]
    conv_rev = _convolve(q_rev,h,'full')
    right = conv_rev[off: off+n]
    grad = source - 2*p0*h - (left+right)
    return grad
```

def _lbgfb_smooth(h, n, s, beta, lam, maxiter, time_limit):
    global _OPT
    _OPT = {'beta': beta, 'lam': lam, 's': s, 'time_limit': time_limit, 'calls':0}

    if hasattr(res,'x'): return project(res.x)
```

Implement projection robust:
```
def project_feasible(x, s):
    n=x.size
    x=np.asarray(x,float)
    # if n==0...
    lo=np.min(x); hi=np.max(x)
    # If sum of clipped at lo/hi? We need find tau in [min(x)-1, max(x)]? For y=clip(x-tau,0,1). ysum decreasing in tau.
    tau_lo = np.min(x) - 1.0  # ysum=n
    tau_hi = np.max(x)      # ysum=0
    for _ in range(80):
       tau=(tau_lo+tau_hi)/2
       y=np.clip(x-tau,0,1)
       ys=y.sum()
       if ys > s:
          tau_lo = tau
       else:
          tau_hi = tau
    y=np.clip(x-tau_hi,0,1) # maybe tau_lo?
    ysum=y.sum()
    # adjust residual
    r = s - ysum
    if r>0:
       # add to entries with largest slack (1-y), or if none all 1
       idx=np.argsort(1-y)[::-1]
    else: ...
    # Adding full r may exceed if r large; but r after bisection small.
    for idx in idx until <=5:
       room = (1-y[idx]) if r>0 else y[idx]
       delta = np.clip(r, 0, room) if r>0 else np.clip(r, -room, 0)
       y[idx]+=delta; r-=delta
       if abs(r)<1e-15: break
    # if still r nonzero due all saturated? shouldn't.
    return y
```

Define `eval_c(h)`:
```
def c5_value(h):
   n=len(h)
   c=np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')

def _lbgfbs(h0, n, s, beta, lam, maxiter, time_limit):
    global _OPT_STATE
    _OPT_STATE = {...}
    try:
       res = scipy.optimize.minimize(_opt_fun, h0.copy(), jac=_opt_grad, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=(_bound_list(n)), **options)
       x = res.x
    except _TimeStop:
       # L-BFGS-B doesn't return partial; we don't have last x? We can maintain last x in state updated in _opt_fun. Set _LAST_X.

    except Exception:
       x = h0
    return project(x,s)
```

Given h length n. For each p, G[p,:] length 2n-1. We can vectorize by building source and neighbor matrices.
- k indices 0..2n-2 correspond to off n-1. Create d = abs(k-off). shape (2n-1).
- source for sign k>=0: start d. For k>=0 (idx>=off), source=1 if p>=d. For k<0, source=1 if p < n-d.
We can for p loop:
```
d = np.abs(k - off)
S = np.where(k >= off, (p >= d), (p < n - d))
left = h[p-d] where p>=d else 0
right= h[p+d] where p+d<n else 0
G[p]=S - left - right
```
O(n*(2n)) = O(n^2). For n=42 fine. Could optimize with slices:
For each p:
  G[p, off-? ] etc. Simpler loop.
Need for d=0: left and right both h[p] if p>=0 and p<n; formula `p>=d` true for d=0 and p+d<n true, so left=h[p], right=h[p], G=1-2h[p]. Good.

But `np.where` alloc. Could implement with zeros and slices:
```
Gp = zeros; Gp[k>=off & p>=d] =1; but vector.
```
Loop over p no big.

Alternatively use relation: For each lag k, grad = sourceW - 2p? But G for all p maybe via convolution of source weights? We can do p loop.

For n up to maybe 500, n^2=250k, one G compute 250k; per pair recompute 450*250k=112M per sweep, maybe heavy. We can not recompute G every pair for large n; maybe use pair method only for n<=100 or limit. For high n use gradient/L-BFGS. Or update G cheaply after pair:
- Moving h_i by δ_i and h_j by δ_j. G[p,k] depends on h at p±d. It changes by δ_q if q = p±d or p=q? For each moved variable q with delta, it affects G[p,k] for pairs where q = p ± d_k. For each k and p such that |p - (q±|k|)|? That's O(n^2) per variable if update all p,k. But we only need G for variables in future pairs. Could not optimize.

Maybe after each pair update, instead of full recompute G, we recompute c via correlate and G via vectorized all p after each sweep (not each pair). Implement subgradient step:
```
step=0.2
while:
  grad=weighted
  norm=np.linalg.norm(grad)
  if norm<1e-12: break
  dir=-grad/norm  # unit direction
  for t in powers of step (e.g., start 0.5? values 0..1, unit dir movement up to 0.5):
      h2=project(h + t*dir)
      f2=eval
      if f2 < f - 1e-13: accept with new step maybe
      t *=0.5
  if no improve: ...
```
Since dir unit, movement t in h-space. Unit step of 0.1 may be moderate. Start t=0.1. If f does not improve at t=0.1, try 0.05... Good. For projected, maybe. Use loop 25. If no improve at 1e-5, break. This may be slow if tiny progress. But helps polish.

```

- `y = project(h - alpha*grad)` for alpha fixed e.g. 0.5. Then t in [0,1] evaluate `h_t=(1-t)h+t*y` (feasible convex combo). If improvement, accept at t. This avoids many projections. Compute y once. Then line search t.
Algorithm:
```
alpha = 1.0/np.sqrt(n) maybe
while:
  grad=weighted
  y=project(h - alpha*grad)
  if norm(y-h) small: break
  best_t=0,best_f=f
  for t in [1,0.5,0.25,...] maybe also 0.75:
     ht = h + t*(y-h)
     ft=eval
     if ft<best_f: best_t=t
  if best_t>0 and ft<f:
      h=ht; f=ft
  else: alpha*=0.5; if alpha<1e-6 break
  else alpha=min(alpha*1.1, 1)
```
This is a projected gradient with fixed step and backtracking. Good.

`_opt_fun` and `_opt_grad` use `_OPT_STATE`; if a call raises _TimeStop, we catch and use last_x. But last_x may be initial if exception early. We store last_x in objective and gradient. However if time stop occurs after minimize completes? no.

Implementation of `_opt_fun`:
```
def _opt_fun(x):
    st = _OPT_STATE
    st['calls'] += 1
    if st['calls'] % 8 == 0 and time.time() > st['deadline']:
         raise _TimeStop
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    x = np.clip(x,0.0,1.0)  # but L-BFGS-B bounds, okay
    st['last_x'] = x.copy()
    beta=st['beta']
    c=_correlate_full(x)
    if beta <= 1e-6:
       val=float(c.max())
    else:
       m=float(c.max())
       z=(c-m)*beta
       # if beta large some underflow; okay
       expv=np.exp(z)
       val=m + np.log(sum(expv))/beta
    pen=st['lam']*(float(x.sum())-st['s'])**2
    return val+pen
```
If beta* c differences too negative, exp underflow to 0. That's fine. If all underflow except max? max exp0. `np.log(sum)` ok. If c has NaN? no.

def _opt_grad(x):
   st=...
   ...
   weights = ...
   g=_weighted_grad(x, weights)
   g += 2*lam*(x.sum()-s)
   return g
```

Subgradient refine can reuse `_weighted_gradient` with weights top/softmax. Need `weights_from_h`? We'll have a function `_weights_from_c(c,beta=None,topk=None)`:
```
def top_softmax_weights(c, beta=20.0, topk=0):

def subgrad_refine(h,n,s,iters=1000, topk=3, alpha0=None, deadline=None):
   h=project; c=corr; f=c.max(); best...
   alpha = alpha0 or 1.0/np.sqrt(n)
   for it in range(iters):
      if time deadline: break
      # choose topk maybe cycle [1,3,5]
      wk = top_uniform for topk_cycle[it%3]
      grad = weighted_grad(h,wk)
      # if grad norm small break
      y = project(h - alpha*grad)
      # line search t
      best_t=0; best_f=f
      # maybe sample t sequence 1,0.8,0.6,0.4,0.25,0.125,...
      for t in np.concatenate(([0.5,0.25,0.125], [0.75,1.0]))? To not too many.
         ht = h + t*(y-h)
         ft=evaluate_c
         if ft<best_f: best_t=t; best_f=ft
      if best_t>0 and best_f < f - 1e-14:
          h=h+best_t*(y-h); f=best_f; update best; alpha=min(alpha*1.15, 1.0)
      else:
          no_improve +=1; alpha*=0.5
          if alpha < 1e-6: break
          # if no_improve large, random kick
          if no_improve %5 ==0:
             rk = h + rng.normal(0,0.05,n); h=project; f=eval; (accept if best? maybe)
   return best
```

```
def make_sec_cache(n):
    off=n-1; L=2*n-1; cache=[]
    for d in range(n):
       arr=np.zeros(L)
       arr[off] = -2.0
       if d>0:
          arr[off-d]+=1.0; arr[off+d]+=1.0
       cache.append(arr)
    return cache
```
For d=0 arr[off]=-2, off-d=off? If d=0, would add +1 twice to same =>0, but pair i==j skip. For safety if d=0: arr[off]=-2. For d>0, arr[off] remains -2. Good.

Precompute G:
```
def compute_G(h):
 n=len(h); off=n-1; k=np.arange(2*n-1); d=np.abs(k-off)
 G=np.empty((n,2*n-1))
 for i in range(n):
    S=np.zeros(2*n-1)
    pos = k >= off
    neg = ~pos
    S[pos] = (i >= d[pos])
    S[neg] = (i < n - d[neg]) # for d=0 in neg? none because k=off pos.
    left=np.zeros(2*n-1); right=np.zeros(2*n-1)
    idx_left = (i >= d)
    if any: left[idx_left]=h[i-d[idx_left]]
    idx_right = (i + d < n)
    if any: right[idx_right]=h[i+d[idx_right]]
    G[i] = S - left - right
 return G
```
But `h[i-d[idx_left]]` works if d is array of ints length L. idx_left boolean; `d[idx_left]` int array. For d=0 pos, left/right both h[i]. Good.
Potential issue for neg with d=0? none. For pos k off d0 S true. For neg none. For pos with d maybe >i, S false; left false; right maybe true? Example k=2,i=0: source [2..], not include; but quadratic term as first? For k>=0, c includes A_{i+k}(1-A_i); if i not source, it can still be target for pair? If k=2,i=0, target i=0 valid; derivative wrt target h_0: -A_2. Formula S=0, left i>=2 false, right i+d=2<n => -h[2]. Good. For k=2,i=5, source true, left -h3, right -h7. Good.
For neg with d, source [0,n-d-1]; target after? derivative includes -left if h_{j-d}? For k<0, c=∑_{i=0}^{n-d-1} A_i(1-A_{i+d}). Derivative wrt j: source if j<n-d. Quadratic if j as second? if j>=d term A_{j-d}A_j => -h[j-d]; as first if j+d<n => -h[j+d]. Our left/right same. Source for neg: j<n-d, i.e. j+d<n. Good. Formula `i < n-d` for neg. Good.

def _correlate_full(h):
    n=len(h)
    if n <= 1200:
       return np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
    # FFT fallback
    N=1
    L=2*n-1
    while N < L: N*=2
    # compute correlate a,b = ifft(ft(a)*conj(ft(b)))? Need align. Use signal.fftconvolve?
    try:
       from scipy.signal import fftconvolve

    except Exception:
       return np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
```

Implement `adjust_sum(h,s)`:
```
def adjust_sum(h,s):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    for _ in range(8):
        r = s - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(r) < 1e-13:
            break
        if abs(r) > 1e-5:
            return project_feasible(h,s)
        if r > 0:
            # add to index with largest room
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h))
        room = (1.0 - h[idx]) if r > 0 else h[idx]
        if room <= 0:
            # distribute
            return project_feasible(h,s)
        delta = r if abs(r) <= room else np.copysign(room, r)
        h[idx] += delta
        h[idx] = min(1.0, max(0.0, h[idx]))
    return h
```
If r small but room <r? not possible if r tiny. Good.

`project_feasible`:
Bisection range. Careful if x has inf/nan. Use np.nan_to_num?
```
def project_feasible(x,s):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float).copy()
    n=x.size
    if n==0: return x
    x=np.clip(x,0,1)
    # initial sum
    cur=float(np.sum(x))
    if abs(cur-s) < 1e-13: return adjust? maybe.
    lo = -1.0  # tau such that y=n?
    hi = 1.0   # y=0? Actually x in [0,1], tau from -1 to 1 covers sum from n to 0.
    # More generally lo=min(x)-1, hi=max(x)
    lo=float(np.min(x)) - 1.0
    hi=float(np.max(x))
    # ensure if sum at lo? At lo, y=clip(x-lo,0,1)=1 all (sum n); at hi, y=0 all. If s in [0,n]
    for _ in range(60):
       tau=0.5*(lo+hi)
       y=np.clip(x-tau,0,1)
       ys=float(np.sum(y))
       if ys > s:
          lo=tau
       else:
          hi=tau
    tau=0.5*(lo+hi)
    y=np.clip(x-tau,0,1)
    return adjust_sum(y,s)
```

```
def _weighted_gradient(h, w):
   n=len(h); off=n-1; L=2*n-1
   p_pos = w[off:off+n].copy()  # d0..n-1
   p_neg = np.zeros(n)

   d = np.arange(1,n)

Let's derive array slice. w length L, off=n-1. We need p_neg[1]=w[off-1], p_neg[2]=w[off-2], ..., p_neg[n-1]=w[0]. So `p_neg[1:] = w[0:off][::-1]` (w[0:off] length n-1). Yes.
`p_pos = w[off:off+n]` length n.
`q = np.zeros(n); if n>1: q[1:] = p_pos[1:] + p_neg[1:]`
`source = np.cumsum(p_pos).astype(float)`
`neg_cum = np.cumsum(p_neg)`
`source = source + neg_cum[n - np.arange(n) - 1]`
Check j=0: index n-1; j=n-1:0. Good.
`p0=p_pos[0]`

`left = np.convolve(q_vec, h, mode='full')[:n]`
`q_rev = q_vec[::-1]`
`right = np.convolve(q_rev, h, mode='full')[off:off+n]`
`grad = source - 2.0*p0*h - (left+right)
return grad`

```
def _opt_grad(x):
 st=_OPT_STATE; st['calls']+=1; if ... raise
 x=np.asarray(x,float); x=np.clip(x,0,1); st['last_x']=x.copy()
 c=_correlate_full(x)
 beta=st['beta']
 if beta <=1e-8:
    m=float(c.max()); active=(c>=m-1e-12); w=np.zeros_like(c); w[active]=1.0; w/=w.sum()
 else:
    m=float(c.max()); z=(c-m)*beta; expv=np.exp(z); w=expv/np.sum(expv)
 g=_weighted_gradient(x,w)
 g += 2.0*st['lam']*(float(np.sum(x))-st['s'])
 return g
```

def lbgfbsmooth(h,n,s,beta,lam,maxiter,deadline):
   global _OPT_STATE
   h=np.asarray(h,float).copy(); h=project_feasible(h,s)
   _OPT_STATE={'beta':beta,'lam':lam,'s':s,'deadline':deadline,'calls':0,'last_x':h.copy()}
   bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
   try:
       res=scipy.optimize.minimize(_opt_fun, h.copy(), jac=_opt_grad, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'maxcor':10,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-10,'maxfun':maxiter*20})
       x=res.x if res.success else res.x
   except _TimeStop:
       x=_OPT_STATE['last_x']
   except Exception:
       x=h
   if x is None: x=h
   return project_feasible(x,s)
```

```
def subgradient_refine(h,n,s,deadline,max_iters,rng):
   ...
   top_cycle=[1,2,4,8]
   alpha=1.0/np.sqrt(max(n,1))
   stagn=0
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if time.time()>deadline: break
      wk=top_uniform_weights(c, top_cycle[it%len])
      g=_weighted_gradient(h,wk)
      ng=float(np.linalg.norm(g))
      if ng < 1e-12: break
      y=project_feasible(h - alpha*g, s)
      # line search
      tvals = [1.0,0.8,0.6,0.4,0.25,0.125,0.0625,0.03125,0.015625]
      best_t=0; best_f=f
      for t in tvals:
         ht=h + t*(y-h)
         ft=float(_correlate_full(ht).max())
         if ft < best_f - 1e-14:
            best_f=ft; best_t=t
      if best_t>0:
         h=h + best_t*(y-h); f=best_f
         if f < best_f_best: update
         alpha=min(alpha*1.15, 0.8)
         stagn=0
      else:
         stagn+=1
         alpha*=0.6
         if alpha < 1e-7:
             # random kick
             sigma=0.02
             hk=project_feasible(h + rng.normal(0,sigma,n), s)
             fk=float(_correlate_full(hk).max())
             if fk < f: h=hk; f=fk
             if fk < best_f: update
         if stagn > 50: break
   return best_h,best_f
```

`pair_coordinate_descent(h,n,s,rng,deadline,m=31,max_sweeps=200):`
```
def pair_coordinate_descent(...):
 h=project_feasible(h,s); n=len(h); c=correlate; cur_f=c.max(); best_h=h.copy(); best_f=cur_f
 if n <2: return best_h,best_f
 G=compute_G(h)
 sec=make_sec_cache(n)
 sweeps=0
 stable=0
 for sweeps in range(max_sweeps):
    if time.time()>deadline: break
    pairs = [(i,j) for i in range(n) for j in range(i+1,n)]
    rng.shuffle(pairs)
    improved=0
    for i,j in pairs:
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       # if current f already <=best? no
       low = max(h[i]-1.0, -h[j]); high = min(h[i], 1.0-h[j])
       if high - low < 1e-14: continue
       # if both variables identical and no active? could still; skip if abs<1e-16 and bounds internal? no.
       d=abs(i-j)
       L = G[j] - G[i]
       sc = sec[d]
       # candidate grid
       if d == 0: continue
       # We can first evaluate 0 and endpoints? grid includes.
       cand = np.linspace(low, high, m)
       # Ensure 0 included if within
       if low < 0 < high and not np.any(np.abs(cand)<1e-12):
           cand = np.concatenate((cand, [0.0])); cand.sort()
       t2 = cand*cand
       # To avoid large matrix if m maybe >50, okay.
       vals = c[None,:] + cand[:,None]*L[None,:] + t2[:,None]*sc[None,:]
       fv = vals.max(axis=1)
       imin = int(np.argmin(fv))
       t_best = cand[imin]; f_best = fv[imin]
       # If best near 0 and f_best not improve, maybe skip refinement.
       if f_best < cur_f - 1e-12:
          # refine around t_best
          stepsize = (high-low)/max(m-1,1)
          lo2 = max(low, t_best - 3*stepsize)
          hi2 = min(high, t_best + 3*stepsize)
          if hi2 > lo2:
             cand2 = np.linspace(lo2, hi2, 31)
             # include current best and 0 if in interval
             cand2 = np.unique(np.concatenate((cand2, [t_best] + ([0.0] if lo2<0<hi2 else []))))
             t22 = cand2*cand2
             vals2 = c[None,:] + cand2[:,None]*L + t22[:,None]*sc
             fv2=vals2.max(axis=1)
             imin2=int(np.argmin(fv2)); t_best=cand2[imin2]; f_best=fv2[imin2]
       if f_best < cur_f - 1e-12:
          # apply
          h[i] -= t_best; h[j] += t_best
          # clamp tiny
          if h[i] < 0 and h[i] > -1e-12: h[i]=0.0
          elif h[i] >1 and h[i] <1+1e-12: h[i]=1.0
          if h[j] < 0 and h[j] > -1e-12: h[j]=0.0
          elif h[j] >1 and h[j] <1+1e-12: h[j]=1.0
          h = np.clip(h,0,1)
          h = adjust_sum(h,s)
          c = _correlate_full(h)
          cur_f = float(c.max())
          if cur_f < best_f - 1e-12:
              best_f=cur_f; best_h=h.copy()
          G = compute_G(h)
          improved +=1
    if improved ==0:
       stable +=1
       # optional random perturbation if not too many
       if stable %5 ==1 and time.time() < deadline:
           hk = project_feasible(h + rng.normal(0,0.03,n), s)
           ck=correlate; fk=ck.max()
           if fk < best_f - 1e-12:
              # restart from hk? But current h maybe not best. We can set h=hk,c=ck,cur_f=fk, recompute G and continue, even if fk>best_f? To escape.
           else:
              # try kick if it improves current but not best
              if fk < cur_f:
                 h=hk; c=ck; cur_f=fk; G=compute_G(h)
                 stable=0
           if fk >= cur_f:
              # no kick
              if stable > 8: break
              stable=0? maybe
       else:
           stable=0
    else:
       stable=0
 return best_h,best_f
```
This is messy. Simpler: pair sweep accepts only immediate improvements. If no improvement in a sweep, break. Then outer add random restarts separately. No anneal. That may be enough and deterministic. For escaping, we can call pair from multiple randomized initial candidates. So pair function can break on no improved sweep.

`upsample_feasible`:
```
def upsample_feasible(h, new_n):
   old_n=len(h); new_s=new_n/2.0
   if new_n == old_n: return project_feasible(h,new_s)
   x_old=(np.arange(old_n)+0.5)*(2.0/old_n)
   x_new=(np.arange(new_n)+0.5)*(2.0/new_n)
   y=np.interp(x_new, x_old, h, left=0.0, right=0.0)
   # maybe preserve endpoints? h values not boundary.
   return project_feasible(y,new_s)
```

def construct_initial(n, seed):
    rng = default_rng(seed)
    # combine cosine with random
    x = np.linspace(0,2,n,endpoint=False) + 1/n
    c1 = 0.5 + 0.5*np.cos(np.pi*x)
    c2 = 0.5 + 0.5*np.sin(2*np.pi*x)
    rnd = 0.5 + 0.35*rng.normal(0,1,n)
    h = (c1 + c2 + rnd)/3
    return project_feasible(h,n/2)

Now, code details for `compute_G` performance: Use vector masks each i. Could optimize by precomputing `d` and `pos`, and slices.
```
def compute_G(h):
    n=len(h); off=n-1; L=2*n-1
    d=np.abs(np.arange(L)-off)
    pos=np.arange(L)>=off
    G=np.empty((n,L))
    for i in range(n):
       S=np.zeros(L)
       S[pos] = i >= d[pos]
       S[~pos] = i < (n - d[~pos])
       # left/right
       leftd = d
       mask_left = i +? Actually p>=d
       idx = i - d
       mask = idx >=0
       left = np.zeros(L); left[mask] = h[idx[mask]]
       idx2 = i + d
       mask2 = idx2 < n
       right = np.zeros(L); right[mask2] = h[idx2[mask2]]
       G[i] = S - left - right
    return G
```

Let's write:
```
def pair_coordinate_descent(h, n, s, rng, deadline, max_sweeps=100, grid=31):
    h = project_feasible(h, s)
    c = _correlate_full(h)
    cur_f = float(c.max())
    best_h = h.copy(); best_f = cur_f
    if n < 2: return best_h,best_f
    G = compute_G(h)
    sec = make_sec_cache(n)
    for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
        if time.time() > deadline: break
        pairs = [(i,j) for i in range(n) for j in range(i+1,n)]
        if rng.size: rng.shuffle(pairs)
        improved = 0
        for i,j in pairs:
            if time.time() > deadline: break
            hi = float(h[i]); hj=float(h[j])
            low = max(hi-1.0, -hj)
            high = min(hi, 1.0-hj)
            if high - low < 1e-13: continue
            d = abs(i-j)
            Lvec = G[j] - G[i]
            sc = sec[d]
            # Quick upper bound? If hi==hj and not near active? Could still.
            cand = np.linspace(low, high, grid)
            if low < 0.0 < high:
                if abs(cand[0])>1e-12 and abs(cand[-1])>1e-12:
                    cand = np.concatenate((cand, np.array([0.0])))
            t2 = cand*cand
            # To avoid integer broadcasting? cand float
            vals = c[None, :] + cand[:, None] * Lvec[None, :] + t2[:, None] * sc[None, :]
            fv = vals.max(axis=1)
            imin = int(np.argmin(fv))
            t_best = float(cand[imin]); f_best = float(fv[imin])
            if f_best >= cur_f - 1e-12:
                continue
            # refine
            stepsize = (high - low) / max(grid - 1, 1)
            lo2 = max(low, t_best - 3.0*stepsize)
            hi2 = min(high, t_best + 3.0*stepsize)
            if hi2 > lo2 + 1e-15:
                cand2 = np.linspace(lo2, hi2, 25)
                extras = [t_best]
                if lo2 < 0.0 < hi2: extras.append(0.0)
                cand2 = np.unique(np.concatenate((cand2, np.array(extras, dtype=float))))
                t22 = cand2*cand2
                vals2 = c[None, :] + cand2[:, None]*Lvec[None, :] + t22[:, None]*sc[None, :]
                fv2 = vals2.max(axis=1)
                imin2 = int(np.argmin(fv2)); t_best = float(cand2[imin2]); f_best=float(fv2[imin2])
            if f_best < cur_f - 1e-12:
                old_h = h.copy(); old_c = c.copy(); old_f = cur_f
                h[i] = hi - t_best
                h[j] = hj + t_best
                # clamp small
                h[np.abs(h) < 1e-15] = 0.0
                h[np.abs(h-1.0) < 1e-15] = 1.0
                h = np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
                h = adjust_sum(h,s)
                c_new = _correlate_full(h)
                f_new = float(c_new.max())
                if f_new < old_f - 1e-12:
                    c = c_new; cur_f = f_new
                    if f_new < best_f - 1e-12:
                       best_f = f_new; best_h = h.copy()
                    G = compute_G(h)
                    improved += 1
                else:
                    h = old_h; c = old_c; cur_f = old_f
        if improved == 0:
            break
    return best_h, best_f
```

```
def subgradient_refine(h, n, s, rng, deadline, max_iters=500):
   h=project; c=correlate; cur_f=...; best...
   alpha=1.0/np.sqrt(n)
   top_cycle=[1,2,4,8]
   stagn=0
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if time>deadline: break
      # maybe update c if h changed? maintain
      wk=top_uniform_weights(c, top_cycle[it%len])
      g=_weighted_gradient(h,wk)
      ng=np.linalg.norm(g)
      if ng <1e-13: break
      y=project_feasible(h - alpha*g, s)
      diff = y-h
      nd = np.linalg.norm(diff)
      if nd <1e-14:
         alpha*=0.5; if alpha<1e-8: break; continue
      best_t=0; best_info=None
      for t in (1.0,0.8,0.6,0.5,0.4,0.3,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.025,0.0125):
         ht=h + t*diff
         ct=correlate
         ft=ct.max()
         if ft < cur_f - 1e-13:
            best_t=t; best_info=(ht,ct,ft)
      if best_info:
         h,c,cur_f=best_info
         if cur_f < best_f: update
         alpha=min(alpha*1.15, 0.5)
         stagn=0
      else:
         stagn+=1; alpha*=0.5
         if alpha < 1e-7:
            # random kick
            hk=project_feasible(h + rng.normal(0, max(0.01, 0.05/np.sqrt(n)), n), s)
            ck=correlate; fk=ck.max()
            if fk < best_f: best update
            if fk < cur_f:
               h=hk; c=ck; cur_f=fk; alpha=0.05; stagn=0
            else:
               if stagn > 30: break
               alpha=0.02; stagn=0
   return best_h,best_f
```
Line search t sequence too many (11) each iter. For n=42, 11*1764*500 ~10M, okay. Could reduce to 8. Use [1,0.7,0.5,0.3,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02]. Good.

`top_uniform_weights`:
```
def top_uniform_weights(c, topk):
    m=c.shape[0]
    if topk <=1 or topk>=m:
       w=np.zeros(m); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0; return w
    idx=np.argpartition(c, -topk)[-topk:]
    w=np.zeros(m); w[idx]=1.0/topk; return w
```
If topk >=m uniform? But top_cycle small. For topk=1, argmax.

In subgradient, if multiple active, top1 arbitrary could cause zigzag. Maybe use [2,4,8,16]. Use cycle [2,4,8,16]. Good.

Now, random kicks in subgrad can produce feasible but not necessarily improve; only update if fk<best or <cur. Good.

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t_start=time.time(); deadline=t_start+float(budget_s)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    init = globals().get('initial_h_values', None)
    best_h=None; best_C=1e100; best_n=0
    if init is not None:
       h0=np.asarray(init, dtype=float).copy()
       if h0.ndim !=1: h0=h0.ravel()
       n0=int(h0.size)
       if n0 <2:
          n0=42; h0=np.full(n0,0.5)
       s0=0.5*n0
       h0=project_feasible(h0,s0)
       C0,c0=evaluate_solution(h0)
       best_h=h0.copy(); best_C=float(C0); best_n=n0
    else:
       base_n=42; s=base_n/2.0
       h0=construct_initial_no_parent(base_n,rng)
       C0=...
       best...
    # quick local improvement base
    n_base=best_n; s_base=best_n/2.0
    # if time left
    if time.time() < deadline - 5:
       local_dead = min(deadline, time.time() + max(60.0, 0.35*float(budget_s)))
       # improve candidate with deadline maybe pair+lbfgs
       h2, f2 = improve_candidate(best_h, n_base, s_base, rng, local_dead, heavy=True)
       C2 = f2*2.0/n_base
       if C2 < best_C: update
    # random restarts at base using small deadlines
    restarts=4
    for r in range(restarts):
       if time.time() > deadline - 30: break
       # generate perturbation of best (or base if same)
       sigma = [0.03,0.08,0.15,0.3][r%4]
       z = rng.normal(0, sigma, best_n)
       z -= z.mean()
       h_r = project_feasible(best_h + z, best_n/2)
       alloc = min(120.0, max(30.0, 0.07*float(budget_s)))
       local_dead = min(deadline, time.time()+alloc)
       h2,f2=improve_candidate(h_r,best_n,best_n/2,rng,local_dead,heavy=False)
       C2=f2*2/best_n; update
    # high sizes
    sizes=[]
    base = best_n
    # Generate multiples if base>0
    muls=[2,3,4,5]
    for m in muls:
       sz=base*m
       if 2<=sz<=400 and sz not in sizes and sz!=base: sizes.append(sz)
    # also if base<60 and 84 not? maybe add 84/120/168
    for cand in [60, 84, 120, 168, 240]:
       if 2<=cand<=400 and cand not in sizes: sizes.append(cand)
    sizes=sorted(set(sizes))
    # Limit high sizes if base large? keep <=400
    for sz in sizes:
       if time.time() > deadline - 60: break
       # choose current best source: if best_n <= sz, use best_h; else downsample? upsample works both directions.
       cand_h = upsample_feasible(best_h, sz)
       alloc = min(160.0, max(60.0, 0.10*float(budget_s)))
       local_dead = min(deadline, time.time()+alloc)
       h2,f2=improve_candidate(cand_h, sz, sz/2.0, rng, local_dead, heavy=False)
       C2=f2*2.0/sz
       if C2 < best_C: update
    # final quick polish of best with pair/subgradient using remaining 60s
    if time.time() < deadline - 2:
       s_final=best_n/2.0
       h_final=project_feasible(best_h,s_final)
       # pair small if <=100
       if best_n <=100:
          hpol, fpol = pair_coordinate_descent(h_final,best_n,s_final,rng,deadline,max_sweeps=20,grid=25)
       else:
          hpol, fpol = subgradient_refine(h_final,best_n,s_final,rng,deadline,max_iters=200)
       Cpol=fpol*2.0/best_n
       if Cpol < best_C: h_final=hpol
       else: h_final=project_feasible(best_h,s_final)
    # ensure final feasible sum
    h_final = adjust_sum(np.asarray(h_final,float), best_n/2.0)
    h_final = np.clip(h_final,0,1)
    C_final = float(evaluate_solution(h_final)[0])
    # If due numerical C_final slightly > best_C but best_h better, use best_h? We'll choose min evaluated.
    return h_final, C_final, int(best_n)

```
def construct_initial_no_parent(n, rng):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/n)
    h1=0.5+0.5*np.cos(np.pi*x)
    h2=0.5+0.5*np.sin(2*np.pi*x)
    h3=0.5+0.5*np.sin(4*np.pi*x)
    rnd = 0.5 + 0.3*rng.normal(0,1,n)
    h=(h1+h2+h3+rnd)/4
    h=project_feasible(h,n/2.0)
    # binary random alt
    h4=rng.integers(0,2,n)*1.0
    idx=np.argsort(h4)
    # choose? maybe skip
    return h
```

```
def improve_candidate(h, n, s, rng, deadline, heavy=False):
    h=project_feasible(h,s); c=correlate; cur_f=float(c.max()); best_h=h.copy(); best_f=cur_f
    # pair
    if n <= (100 if heavy else 70):
       max_sw = 80 if n<=60 else 30
       if heavy: max_sw = 150 if n<=60 else 50
       h2,f2=pair_coordinate_descent(h,n,s,rng,deadline,max_sweeps=max_sw,grid=31 if n<=60 else 21)
       if f2 < best_f: best_h=h2; best_f=f2
    # L-BFGS if time
    if time.time() < deadline - 10:
       beta_list = [4.0, 12.0, 40.0, 120.0] if heavy else [6.0, 20.0]
       maxiter = 250 if n<=80 else 120 if n<=160 else 60
       if heavy and n<=42: maxiter=400
       for beta in beta_list:
          if time.time() > deadline - 5: break
          # allocate fraction remaining; at least 20s
          remaining = deadline - time.time()
          alloc = min(remaining*0.3, 80.0 if heavy else 40.0)
          if alloc < 5: break
          locald = time.time()+alloc
          h3 = lbgfbsmooth(best_h, n, s, beta, lam=150.0, maxiter=maxiter, deadline=locald)
          C3raw = float(_correlate_full(h3).max())
          if C3raw < best_f - 1e-12:
              best_h = project_feasible(h3,n? wait s? n implicit; need n=s*2. Use project(h3,n?); adjust)
              best_f = C3raw
    # subgradient
    if time.time() < deadline - 10 and n>=2:
       locald = min(deadline, time.time() + (60 if heavy else 30))
       h4,f4=subgradient_refine(best_h,n,s,rng,locald,max_iters=400 if n<=80 else 150)
       if f4 < best_f: best_h=h4; best_f=f4
    # pair again
    if n <= (100 if heavy else 70) and time.time() < deadline - 2:
       h5,f5=pair_coordinate_descent(best_h,n,s,rng,deadline,max_sweeps=30 if n<=60 else 15, grid=25)
       if f5 < best_f: best_h=h5; best_f=f5
    return project_feasible(best_h,s), best_f
```

Now, let's examine the weighted gradient formula in L-BFGS-B more thoroughly. We use c from full correlate weights p. But in logsum objective, c values are for `np.correlate(h,1-h)`. The derivative of c_k w.r.t h exactly as G[p,k]. We tested. The source weights and neighbor convolve formula should match G? Let's derive sourceW for positive and negative with p_pos/neg. For index j, sourceW = sum_{k>=0, j>=k} p_pos[k] + sum_{k<0, j<n-|k|} p_neg[|k|].
Our pos_cum[j]=∑_{k=0}^j p_pos[k]. neg_cum[n-j-1]=∑_{l=0}^{n-j-1} p_neg[l]; p_neg[0]=0, so l=1..n-j-1. correct.
Neighbor: For each distance d>0, contribution q_d=h[j-d] if j>=d + h[j+d] if j+d<n. left = conv(q_vec,h) where q_vec[d]=q_d. out[j]=∑_{t} q_vec[t]h[j-t]. For t=d≥1, if j>=d, h[j-d]. For t=0 0. Are there terms t>j? h negative out of range zero in linear convolve? np.convolve zero-pads, so yes. right = conv(q_rev,h) at off+j. q_rev[t] = q_vec[Off? Let's re-derive. q_rev = q_vec[::-1] length n, index t corresponds to q_vec[n-1-t]. out_rev[off+j]=∑_{t=0}^{n-1} q_rev[t] h[off+j - t] where h index valid 0..n-1. Let d? If we want term h[j+d], set off+j - t = j+d => t = off-d = n-1-d. q_rev[n-1-d] = q_vec[d] = q_d. As d ranges 1..n-1-j? For t valid, d≤n-1-j => j+d≤n-1. Good. So right =∑_{d=1}^{n-1-j} q_d h[j+d]. Good. Neighbor sum correct.
For d=0, we subtract 2p0h; neighbor excludes d=0. Good.

But for negative k, source uses p_neg[l]; neighbor distance d uses q=p_pos+d+p_neg+d. The neighbor terms from p_pos[d] and p_neg[d] both identical, so q sum. Good.

Now, in `_opt_grad`, the penalty derivative: if objective = logsum + lam*(sum-s)^2, grad = grad_logsum + 2 lam (sum-s) * ones. Our g is derivative of logsum (not scaled by? logsum val = m+log(sumexp)/beta; gradient ∑p ∇c. Correct). Good.

L-BFGS-B may evaluate at x with sum far due bounds? It starts feasible. Good.

```python
import time
import numpy as np
import scipy.optimize

_OPT_STATE = None


class _TimeStop(Exception):
    pass


def _correlate_full(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")


def evaluate_solution(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = int(h.size)
    c = _correlate_full(h)
    return float(float(c.max()) * (2.0 / n)), c


def adjust_sum(h, s):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    for _ in range(8):
        r = float(s - float(np.sum(h)))
        if abs(r) < 1e-13:
            break
        if abs(r) > 1e-5:
            return project_feasible(h, s)
        if r > 0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            room = float(1.0 - h[idx])
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h))
            room = float(h[idx])
        if room <= 0.0:
            return project_feasible(h, s)
        delta = r
        if abs(delta) > room:
            delta = room if r > 0 else -room
        h[idx] += delta
        if h[idx] < 0.0:
            h[idx] = 0.0
        elif h[idx] > 1.0:
            h[idx] = 1.0
    return h


def project_feasible(x, s):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).copy()
    n = int(x.size)
    if n == 0:
        return x
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    s = float(s)
    # Range of tau: tau = -1 gives all ones, tau = +1 gives all zeros.
    lo = float(np.min(x)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(x))
    # 60 bisections are enough for float64 on unit-interval data.
    for _ in range(60):
        tau = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        y = np.clip(x - tau, 0.0, 1.0)
        ys = float(np.sum(y))
        if ys > s:
            lo = tau
        else:
            hi = tau
    tau = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    y = np.clip(x - tau, 0.0, 1.0)
    return adjust_sum(y, s)


def construct_initial_no_parent(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    a = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(np.pi * x)
    b = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    r = 0.5 + 0.25 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
    h = 0.333333 * a + 0.333333 * b + 0.333334 * r
    return project_feasible(h, 0.5 * n)


def upsample_feasible(h, new_n):
    old_n = int(np.asarray(h).size)
    new_n = int(new_n)
    if new_n == old_n:
        return project_feasible(h, 0.5 * new_n)
    if new_n <= 0 or old_n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(new_n, dtype=float)
    x_old = (np.arange(old_n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / old_n)
    x_new = (np.arange(new_n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / new_n)
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h, left=0.0, right=0.0)
    return project_feasible(y, 0.5 * new_n)


def compute_G(h):
    n = int(len(h))
    off = n - 1
    L = 2 * n - 1
    k = np.arange(L, dtype=int)
    d = np.abs(k - off)
    pos = k >= off
    neg = ~pos
    G = np.empty((n, L), dtype=float)
    for i in range(n):
        S = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
        S[pos] = (i >= d[pos]).astype(float)
        S[neg] = (i < (n - d[neg])).astype(float)
        left = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
        idx = i - d
        m = idx >= 0
        if m.any():
            left[m] = h[idx[m]]
        right = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
        idx2 = i + d
        m2 = idx2 < n
        if m2.any():
            right[m2] = h[idx2[m2]]
        G[i, :] = S - left - right
    return G


def make_sec_cache(n):
    n = int(n)
    off = n - 1
    L = 2 * n - 1
    cache = []
    for d in range(n):
        a = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
        a[off] = -2.0
        if d > 0:
            a[off - d] += 1.0
            a[off + d] += 1.0
        cache.append(a)
    return cache


def pair_coordinate_descent(h, n, s, rng, deadline, max_sweeps=100, grid=31):
    h = project_feasible(h, s)
    c = _correlate_full(h)
    cur_f = float(c.max())
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_f = cur_f
    n = int(n)
    if n < 2:
        return best_h, best_f
    G = compute_G(h)
    sec_cache = make_sec_cache(n)
    grid = max(5, int(grid))

    for _sweep in range(max_sweeps):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        pairs = [(i, j) for i in range(n) for j in range(i + 1, n)]
        try:
            rng.shuffle(pairs)
        except Exception:
            pass
        improved = 0
        for i, j in pairs:
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            hi = float(h[i])
            hj = float(h[j])
            low = max(hi - 1.0, -hj)
            high = min(hi, 1.0 - hj)
            if high - low < 1e-13:
                continue
            d = abs(i - j)
            Lvec = G[j] - G[i]
            sc = sec_cache[d]

            cand = np.linspace(low, high, grid)
            if low < 0.0 < high:
                if abs(cand[0]) > 1e-12 and abs(cand[-1]) > 1e-12:
                    cand = np.concatenate((cand, np.array([0.0], dtype=float)))
            t2 = cand * cand
            vals = (
                c[None, :]
                + cand[:, None] * Lvec[None, :]
                + t2[:, None] * sc[None, :]
            )
            fv = vals.max(axis=1)
            imin = int(np.argmin(fv))
            t_best = float(cand[imin])
            f_best = float(fv[imin])
            if f_best >= cur_f - 1e-12:
                continue

            stepsize = (high - low) / max(grid - 1, 1)
            lo2 = max(low, t_best - 3.0 * stepsize)
            hi2 = min(high, t_best + 3.0 * stepsize)
            if hi2 > lo2 + 1e-15:
                cand2 = np.linspace(lo2, hi2, 25)
                extras = [t_best]
                if lo2 < 0.0 < hi2:
                    extras.append(0.0)
                cand2 = np.unique(np.concatenate((cand2, np.array(extras, dtype=float))))
                t22 = cand2 * cand2
                vals2 = (
                    c[None, :]
                    + cand2[:, None] * Lvec[None, :]
                    + t22[:, None] * sc[None, :]
                )
                fv2 = vals2.max(axis=1)
                imin2 = int(np.argmin(fv2))
                t_best = float(cand2[imin2])
                f_best = float(fv2[imin2])

            if f_best < cur_f - 1e-12:
                old_h = h.copy()
                old_c = c.copy()
                old_f = cur_f
                h[i] = hi - t_best
                h[j] = hj + t_best
                h[np.abs(h) < 1e-15] = 0.0
                h[np.abs(h - 1.0) < 1e-15] = 1.0
                h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
                h = adjust_sum(h, s)
                c_new = _correlate_full(h)
                f_new = float(c_new.max())
                if f_new < old_f - 1e-12:
                    h, c, cur_f = h, c_new, f_new
                    if f_new < best_f - 1e-12:
                        best_f = f_new
                        best_h = h.copy()
                    G = compute_G(h)
                    improved += 1
                else:
                    h, c, cur_f = old_h, old_c, old_f
        if improved == 0:
            break
    return best_h, best_f


def top_uniform_weights(c, topk):
    m = int(c.size)
    topk = int(topk)
    if topk <= 0 or topk >= m:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / m, dtype=float)
    if topk == 1:
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        w = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
        w[idx] = 1.0
        return w
    idx = np.argpartition(c, -topk)[-topk:]
    w = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
    w[idx] = 1.0 / topk
    return w


def _weighted_gradient(h, w):
    n = int(len(h))
    off = n - 1
    L = 2 * n - 1
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=float)
    p_pos = w[off:off + n].copy()
    p_neg = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if n > 1:
        p_neg[1:] = w[0:off][::-1].copy()
    q = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if n > 1:
        q[1:] = p_pos[1:] + p_neg[1:]

    source = np.cumsum(p_pos).astype(float)
    neg_cum = np.cumsum(p_neg).astype(float)
    source = source + neg_cum[n - np.arange(n) - 1]

    p0 = float(p_pos[0])
    qvec = q.copy()
    qvec[0] = 0.0
    left = np.convolve(qvec, h, mode="full")[:n]
    qrev = qvec[::-1]
    right = np.convolve(qrev, h, mode="full")[off:off + n]

    grad = source - 2.0 * p0 * h - (left + right)
    return grad


def _opt_fun(x):
    st = _OPT_STATE
    st["calls"] += 1
    if st["calls"] % 8 == 0 and time.time() > st["deadline"]:
        raise _TimeStop
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    st["last_x"] = x.copy()
    c = _correlate_full(x)
    beta = float(st["beta"])
    if beta <= 1e-8:
        val = float(c.max())
    else:
        m = float(c.max())
        z = (c - m) * beta
        ez = np.exp(z)
        val = float(m + np.log(float(np.sum(ez))) / beta)
    s = float(st["s"])
    val += float(st["lam"]) * (float(np.sum(x)) - s) ** 2
    return val


def _opt_grad(x):
    st = _OPT_STATE
    st["calls"] += 1
    if st["calls"] % 8 == 0 and time.time() > st["deadline"]:
        raise _TimeStop
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    st["last_x"] = x.copy()
    c = _correlate_full(x)
    beta = float(st["beta"])
    if beta <= 1e-8:
        m = float(c.max())
        w = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=float)
        w[np.argmax(c)] = 1.0
    else:
        m = float(c.max())
        z = (c - m) * beta
        ez = np.exp(z)
        w = ez / float(np.sum(ez))
    g = _weighted_gradient(x, w)
    s = float(st["s"])
    g += 2.0 * float(st["lam"]) * (float(np.sum(x)) - s)
    return g


def lbgfbsmooth(h, n, s, beta, lam, maxiter, deadline):
    n = int(n)
    h = project_feasible(h, s)
    if n < 2:
        return h
    global _OPT_STATE
    _OPT_STATE = {
        "beta": float(beta),
        "lam": float(lam),
        "s": float(s),
        "deadline": float(deadline),
        "calls": 0,
        "last_x": h.copy(),
    }
    try:
        res = scipy.optimize.minimize(
            _opt_fun,
            h.copy(),
            jac=_opt_grad,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            bounds=[(0.0, 1.0)] * n,
            options={
                "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                "maxcor": 10,
                "ftol": 1e-12,
                "gtol": 1e-10,
                "maxfun": int(maxiter) * 20 + 100,
            },
        )
        x = res.x
    except _TimeStop:
        x = _OPT_STATE["last_x"] if _OPT_STATE is not None else h
    except Exception:
        x = h
    return project_feasible(x, s)


def subgradient_refine(h, n, s, rng, deadline, max_iters=300):
    h = project_feasible(h, s)
    c = _correlate_full(h)
    cur_f = float(c.max())
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_f = cur_f
    n = int(n)
    if n < 2:
        return best_h, best_f
    alpha = 1.0 / np.sqrt(n)
    top_cycle = [2, 4, 8, 16]
    stagnation = 0

    for it in range(int(max_iters)):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        wk = top_uniform_weights(c, top_cycle[it % len(top_cycle)])
        g = _weighted_gradient(h, wk)
        ng = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
        if ng < 1e-13:
            break
        y = project_feasible(h - alpha * g, s)
        diff = y - h
        nd = float(np.linalg.norm(diff))
        if nd < 1e-14:
            alpha *= 0.5
            if alpha < 1e-9:
                break
            continue

        found = None
        for t in (1.0, 0.75, 0.5, 0.35, 0.2, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02):
            ht = h + t * diff
            ct = _correlate_full(ht)
            ft = float(ct.max())
            if ft < cur_f - 1e-13:
                found = (ht, ct, ft)
        if found is not None:
            h, c, cur_f = found
            if cur_f < best_f - 1e-12:
                best_f = cur_f
                best_h = h.copy()
            alpha = min(alpha * 1.15, 0.5)
            stagnation = 0
        else:
            stagnation += 1
            alpha *= 0.55
            if alpha < 1e-8:
                sigma = max(0.01, 0.05 / np.sqrt(n))
                hk = project_feasible(h + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n), s)
                ck = _correlate_full(hk)
                fk = float(ck.max())
                if fk < best_f - 1e-12:
                    best_f = fk
                    best_h = hk.copy()
                if fk < cur_f - 1e-13:
                    h, c, cur_f = hk, ck, fk
                    alpha = 0.05
                    stagnation = 0
                else:
                    if stagnation > 30:
                        break
                    alpha = max(0.02, 0.5 / np.sqrt(n))
                    stagnation = 0
    return best_h, best_f


def improve_candidate(h, n, s, rng, deadline, heavy=False):
    h = project_feasible(h, s)
    n = int(n)
    s = float(s)
    c = _correlate_full(h)
    cur_f = float(c.max())
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_f = cur_f

    if n >= 2:
        pair_max = n <= 100 if heavy else n <= 70
        if pair_max and time.time() < deadline - 1.0:
            if n <= 60:
                max_sw = 160 if heavy else 70
                grid = 31
            elif n <= 100:
                max_sw = 60 if heavy else 30
                grid = 25
            else:
                max_sw = 20
                grid = 21
            h2, f2 = pair_coordinate_descent(h, n, s, rng, deadline, max_sweeps=max_sw, grid=grid)
            if f2 < best_f - 1e-12:
                best_h, best_f = h2, f2

    if n >= 2 and time.time() < deadline - 8.0:
        if heavy:
            beta_list = [4.0, 12.0, 40.0, 120.0]
        else:
            beta_list = [6.0, 25.0]
        if n <= 60:
            maxiter = 400 if heavy else 250
        elif n <= 120:
            maxiter = 250 if heavy else 160
        elif n <= 200:
            maxiter = 140
        else:
            maxiter = 90
        for beta in beta_list:
            if time.time() > deadline - 5.0:
                break
            remaining = deadline - time.time()
            alloc = min(max(3.0, remaining * 0.28), 90.0 if heavy else 45.0)
            local_deadline = time.time() + alloc
            h3 = lbgfbsmooth(best_h, n, s, beta, lam=150.0, maxiter=maxiter, deadline=local_deadline)
            c3 = _correlate_full(h3)
            f3 = float(c3.max())
            if f3 < best_f - 1e-12:
                best_h, best_f = h3, f3

    if n >= 2 and time.time() < deadline - 5.0:
        local_deadline = min(deadline, time.time() + (55.0 if heavy else 30.0))
        maxit = 600 if n <= 60 else (350 if n <= 120 else 160)
        h4, f4 = subgradient_refine(best_h, n, s, rng, local_deadline, max_iters=maxit)
        if f4 < best_f - 1e-12:
            best_h, best_f = h4, f4

    if n >= 2 and n <= (100 if heavy else 70) and time.time() < deadline - 1.0:
        h5, f5 = pair_coordinate_descent(
            best_h, n, s, rng, deadline, max_sweeps=35 if n <= 60 else 18, grid=25
        )
        if f5 < best_f - 1e-12:
            best_h, best_f = h5, f5

    return project_feasible(best_h, s), float(best_f)


def finalize_h(h, s):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    s = float(s)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    h = adjust_sum(h, s)
    if abs(float(np.sum(h)) - s) > 1e-8:
        h = project_feasible(h, s)
        h = adjust_sum(h, s)
    return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.time()
    budget = float(budget_s)
    deadline = t_start + budget
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) if seed is not None else 42)

    best_h = None
    best_C = np.inf
    best_n = 0

    init = globals().get("initial_h_values", None)
    if init is not None:
        h0 = np.asarray(init, dtype=float)
        if h0.ndim != 1:
            h0 = h0.ravel()
        n0 = int(h0.size)
        if n0 < 2:
            n0 = 42
            h0 = np.full(n0, 0.5, dtype=float)
        s0 = 0.5 * n0

        h_adj = np.clip(h0, 0.0, 1.0)
        h_adj = adjust_sum(h_adj, s0)
        c_adj = _correlate_full(h_adj)
        C_adj = float(c_adj.max() * (2.0 / n0))

        h_proj = project_feasible(h0, s0)
        c_proj = _correlate_full(h_proj)
        C_proj = float(c_proj.max() * (2.0 / n0))

        if C_adj <= C_proj + 1e-12:
            best_h, best_C, best_n = h_adj, C_adj, n0
        else:
            best_h, best_C, best_n = h_proj, C_proj, n0
    else:
        n0 = 42
        h0 = construct_initial_no_parent(n0, rng)
        c0 = _correlate_full(h0)
        best_h = h0
        best_C = float(c0.max() * (2.0 / n0))
        best_n = n0

    # Heavy optimization at the base resolution.
    if time.time() < deadline - 5.0:
        base_alloc = max(60.0, 0.35 * budget)
        local_deadline = min(deadline, time.time() + base_alloc)
        h2, f2 = improve_candidate(best_h, best_n, 0.5 * best_n, rng, local_deadline, heavy=True)
        C2 = float(f2 * (2.0 / best_n))
        if C2 < best_C:
            best_h, best_C, best_n = h2, C2, best_n

    # A few randomized restarts at the same resolution.
    restarts = 3
    for r in range(restarts):
        if time.time() > deadline - 40.0:
            break
        sigma = [0.03, 0.09, 0.18][r % 3]
        z = rng.normal(0.0, sigma, int(best_n))
        z -= float(z.mean())
        h_r = project_feasible(best_h + z, 0.5 * best_n)
        alloc = min(max(25.0, 0.06 * budget), 110.0)
        local_deadline = min(deadline, time.time() + alloc)
        h2, f2 = improve_candidate(h_r, best_n, 0.5 * best_n, rng, local_deadline, heavy=False)
        C2 = float(f2 * (2.0 / best_n))
        if C2 < best_C:
            best_h, best_C, best_n = h2, C2, best_n

    # Higher resolutions built from the current best shape.
    base = int(best_n)
    high_candidates = []
    for m in (2, 3, 4):
        sz = base * m
        if 2 <= sz <= 300:
            high_candidates.append(sz)
    for cand in (84, 120, 168, 240):
        if base <= 120 and 2 <= cand <= 300:
            high_candidates.append(cand)
    high_candidates = sorted(set(x for x in high_candidates if x != base))
    high_candidates = high_candidates[:4]

    for sz in high_candidates:
        if time.time() > deadline - 70.0:
            break
        cand_h = upsample_feasible(best_h, int(sz))
        alloc = min(max(35.0, 0.09 * budget), 150.0)
        local_deadline = min(deadline, time.time() + alloc)
        h2, f2 = improve_candidate(cand_h, int(sz), 0.5 * sz, rng, local_deadline, heavy=False)
        C2 = float(f2 * (2.0 / sz))
        if C2 < best_C:
            best_h, best_C, best_n = h2, C2, int(sz)

    # Final short polish of the global best.
    if time.time() < deadline - 2.0:
        hn = int(best_n)
        sn = 0.5 * hn
        if hn <= 100:
            hp, fp = pair_coordinate_descent(best_h, hn, sn, rng, deadline, max_sweeps=25, grid=25)
        else:
            hp, fp = subgradient_refine(best_h, hn, sn, rng, deadline, max_iters=250)
        Cp = float(fp * (2.0 / hn))
        if Cp < best_C:
            best_h, best_C = hp, Cp

    # Produce a numerically feasible final vector and report its exact objective.
    sn = 0.5 * int(best_n)
    final_candidates = []
    final_candidates.append(finalize_h(best_h, sn))
    final_candidates.append(project_feasible(best_h, sn))

    chosen_h = None
    chosen_C = np.inf
    for cand in final_candidates:
        cand = np.clip(np.asarray(cand, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
        Cv, _c = evaluate_solution(cand)
        if np.isfinite(Cv) and Cv < chosen_C:
            chosen_C = float(Cv)
            chosen_h = cand

    if chosen_h is None:
        chosen_h = finalize_h(best_h, sn)
        chosen_C, _ = evaluate_solution(chosen_h)

    return chosen_h, float(chosen_C), int(best_n)
```
